## Cohesion observations (principle (4); not internal conflicts)

- **CO-01** — The unit never points at the design volumes: 0 pointers to docs/volumes/V01–V12 or INDEX.md across its 98 458 words (script count). The title, the reading contract, the one-page summary, §1.1's stage table, §14, §15, §16, §17.1 and the Staged list describe a base-only design. *Where:* title; summary; §1.1; §14; §15; §16; §17.1; Staged list. *Basis:* principle (4): the summary, §1, §14, §15 and §16 must be true of the whole set.
- **CO-02** — §14 places none of the volumes' packages (@plat/buildings, @plat/under, @plat/sky, @plat/evolve, @plat/village, @plat/bridge-client, @plat/clerk, @plat/cli) and its dependency chain omits them (SCOPE-C16). *Where:* §14. *Basis:* census: docs/volumes/V*.md @plat names.
- **CO-03** — §16's milestone table and D-35 stop at M5; the combined order J122 records (V-M0 after M0; M3c beside M3b; after M4: M5 ∥ V-M ∥ M6-E ∥ the plan rung's P0–P1; M6 and M6-U after M5; M6-sky; M-CAL; M-SF) is absent, and the staged engines are 'not priced, by the roadmap' while INDEX.md prices them (SCOPE-C04). *Where:* §16; D-35; D-20; summary. *Basis:* J122 instructs writing the combined order into §16 and INDEX.md.
- **CO-04** — §15.1 tags the VG rows VILLAGE-LATER ('checked as not precluded') and the NG rows STAGE-2 'through an interface'; V07 and V01 now deliver them at build depth. *Where:* §15. *Basis:* —.
- **CO-05** — §0.2's landing tables end at item 101; file g's items 102–113 and file f's items 200–585 have no landing rows (the rewrite adds them as the pass applies them). *Where:* §0.2. *Basis:* f items homed here per the f file's own section order: see f_items_homed_in_unit.

## Elegance candidates in this unit (principle (9); for the canon to rule with an equivalence list)

- **EL-01** — Three copies of the owner-item bar convention (§13.2 l.13229 amendments b, l.13415 amendments c, l.13460 owner rules; J-A63.01): one convention paragraph ('hard unless banded; tier from §13.8; from the milestone §16 names; a staged engine's bars on hook fixtures until the engine lands') cited by every group. *Guard:* UNIFY candidate — equivalence: the three state the same four clauses; the owner-rules copy adds the registry path-test list for amendments c and the declared-shift clause for goldens (keep both as lines of the one convention).
- **EL-02** — Two tier-schedule paragraphs in §13.8 plus per-milestone T-lists in §16's exits: one schedule table (test id or range → CI tier → first milestone), cited by §16's exits. *Guard:* UNIFY candidate — every T-range's tier and milestone must carry over exactly (e.g. T28-12's wings clause from M3a, the plan clause from stage 3's intake).
- **EL-03** — §0.2's three landing tables (items 1–7; 8–31; 32–72 and 101) share one discipline and one column set: one table. *Guard:* UNIFY candidate — the clause-map column exists in all three.
- **EL-04** — §13.9's re-signing lists (4), (5), (7) split by amendment round: one list of calibratable table families. *Guard:* UNIFY candidate — the exclusion 'dossier row sets are not re-signed' stays.
- **EL-05** — §16's effort basis cells carry per-round '+x pw' narration (amendments b; c–e): one itemised basis per line (the numbers kept). *Guard:* shrink (narration) — the line totals and milestone carries unchanged.
- **EL-06** — The summary's two appended 'amendments' paragraphs: folded into the plain-words body by subject. *Guard:* shrink (narration) — every fact they carry kept once.
- **EL-07** — Judgment-calls index: a supersession marker column (J-P16 → J-A37.41; J-A29.01 → J-A50.01/J-A50.21; J-A30.71/72 → J-A20.77; J-A30.22 → J-A30.24; J-A8.21 → J-A8.22) instead of inline '(amended by …)' notes. *Guard:* form change — no row dropped.
- **EL-08** — FTG size enumerations (RungProfile.matched.ftgSize vs Baseline.matched.ftgSize): one enumeration type. *Guard:* UNIFY candidate (SCOPE-C10) — 'CITY' (FTG's timed CITY preset, §13.5 generation-time row) must stay expressible.
